<div className="bible-chapter" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-lang="en" data-translation="nmv" data-translation-id="eng_nmv" data-translation-label="NMV" data-book="DEU" data-book-slug="deuteronomy" data-book-name="Deuteronomy" data-chapter="1" data-verses="959">

<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:1; Joshua 22:7; Numbers 33:17–18"> These &#91;be&#93; the words which Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; spoke unto all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; on this side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; in the wilderness, in the plain over against the Red &#91;sea&#93;, between Pa’ran &#91;place of caverns&#93;, and Tofel &#91;White&#93;, and Lavan &#91;White&#93;, and Hatzerot &#91;A Place in the Wilderness&#93;, and Di-Zahav &#91;Enough Gold&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Numbers 32:8; Numbers 13:26; Deuteronomy 9:23"> (&#91;There are&#93; eleven days’ &#91;journey&#93; from Horev &#91;Desert&#93; by the way of mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93; unto Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93; -Barnea &#91;Holy&#93;.)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Numbers 33:38; Deuteronomy 4:1–2; Numbers 20:1"> And it came to pass in the fortieth year, in the eleventh month, on the first &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; spoke unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, according unto all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had given him in commandment unto them;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 21:21–35; Joshua 12:2–6; Joshua 13:10–12"> After he had slain Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; the king of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, which dwelt in Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93;, and ‘Og &#91;Long-necked&#93; the king of Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;, which dwelt at ‘Ashtarot &#91;Star&#93; in Edre’i &#91;Good Pasture&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:8; Deuteronomy 32:46; Deuteronomy 31:9"> On this side Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, in the land of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, began Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; to declare this law, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 3:1; Numbers 10:11–13; Exodus 19:1–2"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; spoke unto us in Horev &#91;Desert&#93;, saying, You2f have dwelt long enough in this mount:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Deuteronomy 11:24; Joshua 10:40; Amos 2:9"> Turn you, and take your journey, and go to the mount of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, and unto all &#91;the places&#93; near thereunto, in the plain, in the hills, and in the vale, and in the south, and by the sea side, to the land of the Kena’ani &#91;Merchant&#93;, and unto L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;, unto the great river, the river Euphrates (to break forth, to gush).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 15:18; Genesis 12:7; Genesis 26:3–4"> Behold, I have set the land before you: go in and possess the land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) swore unto your fathers, Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, to give unto them and to their seed after them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Exodus 18:18; Numbers 11:17; Numbers 11:11–14"> And I spoke unto you at that time, saying, I am not able to bear you myself alone:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Exodus 32:13; Genesis 22:17"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has multiplied you, and, behold, you2f &#91;are&#93; this day as the stars of heaven for multitude.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 115:14; Genesis 22:17; Numbers 6:27"> (The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of your fathers make you a thousand times so many more as you2f &#91;are&#93;, and bless you, as he has promised you!)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="2 Corinthians 3:5; 1 Kings 3:7–9; 2 Corinthians 2:16"> How can I myself alone bear your cumbrance, and your burden, and your strife?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Exodus 18:21; Acts 1:21–23; Numbers 11:16–17"> Take you wise men, and understanding, and known among your tribes, and I will make them rulers over you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> And you2f answered me, and said, The thing which youi have spoken &#91;is&#93; good &#91;for us&#93; to do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:18; Exodus 18:25–26; Ephesians 4:11"> So I took the chief of your tribes, wise men, and known, and made them heads over you, captains over thousands, and captains over hundreds, and captains over fifties, and captains over tens, and officers among your tribes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="John 7:24; Exodus 22:21; 2 Chronicles 19:6–10"> And I charged your at that time, saying, Hear &#91;the causes&#93; between your brethren, and judge righteously between &#91;every&#93; man and his brother, and the stranger &#91;that is&#93; with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:19; Exodus 18:26; Jacob 2:9"> You2f shall not respect persons in judgment; &#91;but&#93; you2f shall hear the small as well as the great; you2f shall not be afraid of the face of man; for the judgment &#91;is&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;’s: and the cause that is too hard for you, bring &#91;it&#93; unto me, and I will hear it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Deuteronomy 12:32; Deuteronomy 4:5; Acts 20:20"> And I commanded you at that time all the things which you2f should do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:2; Deuteronomy 8:15; Jeremiah 2:6"> And when we departed from Horev &#91;Desert&#93;, we went through all that great and terrible wilderness, which you2f saw by the way of the mountain of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; commanded us; and we came to Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93; -Barnea &#91;Holy&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:7–8"> And I said unto you, You2f are come unto the mountain of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; does give unto us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Tehillim - Psalms 46:1; Numbers 14:8–9"> Behold, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has set the land before youi: go up &#91;and&#93; possess &#91;it&#93;, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of youi fathers has said unto youi; fear not, neither be discouraged.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 13:1–20"> And you2f came near unto me every one of you, and said, We will send men before us, and they shall search us out the land, and bring us word again by what way we must go up, and into what cities we shall come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 13:3–33"> And the saying pleased me well: and I took twelve men of you, one of a tribe:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 13:21–27; Joshua 2:1–2"> And they turned and went up into the mountain, and came unto the valley of Eshkol &#91;Cluster&#93;, and searched it out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And they took of the fruit of the land in their hands, and brought &#91;it&#93; down unto us, and brought us word again, and said, &#91;It is&#93; a good land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; does give us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Numbers 14:1–4; Acts 7:51; Tehillim - Psalms 106:24–25"> Yet you2f would not go up, but rebelled against the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Deuteronomy 9:28; Tehillim - Psalms 106:25; Numbers 14:3"> And you2f murmured in your tents, and said, Because the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) hated us, he has brought us forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, to deliver us into the hand of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, to destroy us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Numbers 13:28–33; Deuteronomy 9:1–2; Joshua 15:14"> Where shall we go up? our brethren have discouraged our heart, saying, The people &#91;is&#93; greater and taller than we; the cities &#91;are&#93; great and walled up to heaven; and moreover we have seen the sons of the ‘Anakim &#91;Longed-Necked&#93; there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Then I said unto you, Dread not, neither be afraid of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Exodus 14:14; Nehemiah 4:20; Romans 8:31"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; which goes before you, he shall fight for you, according to all that he did for you in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; before your eyes;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Isaiah 46:3–4; Acts 13:18; Hosea 11:3–4"> And in the wilderness, where youi have seen how that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; bore youi, as a man does bear his son, in all the way that you2f went, until you2f came into this place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 106:24; Judah 1:5; Hebrews 3:18–19"> Yet in this thing you2f did not believe the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33" data-xref="Numbers 10:33; Tehillim - Psalms 78:14; Nehemiah 9:12"> Who went in the way before you, to search you out a place to pitch your tents &#91;in&#93;, in fire by night, to show you by what way you2f should go, and in a cloud by day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34" data-xref="Numbers 14:22–30; Tehillim - Psalms 95:11; Ezekiel 20:15"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) heard the voice of your words, and was exceedingly angry, and swore, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 95:11"> Surely there shall not one of these men of this evil generation see that good land, which I swore to give unto your fathers,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Numbers 14:24; Joshua 14:6–14; Numbers 32:12"> Save Kalev &#91;Dog&#93; the son of Y’funeh &#91;He Will Face&#93;; he shall see it, and to him will I give the land that he has trampled upon, and to his children, because he has wholly followed the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37" data-xref="Numbers 20:12; Deuteronomy 4:21; Deuteronomy 34:4"> Also the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was angry with me for your sakes, saying, youi also shall not go in there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38" data-xref="Deuteronomy 3:28; Numbers 14:30; Deuteronomy 31:7–8"> &#91;But&#93; Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; the son of Nun &#91;Posterity&#93;, which stands before youi, he shall go in there: encourage him: for he shall cause Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to inherit it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39" data-xref="Isaiah 7:15–16; Numbers 14:3; Numbers 14:31"> Moreover your little ones, which you2f said should be a prey, and your children, which in that day had no knowledge between good and evil, they shall go in there, and unto them will I give it, and they shall possess it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40" data-xref="Numbers 14:25"> But &#91;as for&#93; you, turn you, and take your journey into the wilderness by the way of the Red sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41" data-xref="Numbers 14:39–45; Mishlei - Proverbs 19:3; Numbers 22:34"> Then you2f answered and said unto me, We have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), we will go up and fight, according to all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; commanded us. And when you2f had girded on every man his weapons of war, you2f were ready to go up into the hill.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42" data-xref="Numbers 14:41–43; Joshua 7:8–13; Isaiah 59:1–2"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, Say unto them, Go not up, neither fight; for I &#91;am&#93; not among you; lest you2f be smitten before your enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43" data-xref="Numbers 14:44; Isaiah 63:10; Acts 7:51"> So I spoke unto you; and you2f would not hear, but rebelled against the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and went presumptuously up into the hill.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 118:12; Numbers 14:45; Numbers 21:3"> And the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, which dwelt in that mountain, came out against you, and chased you, as bees do, and destroyed you in Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, &#91;even&#93; unto Hormah &#91;Asylum&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45" data-xref="Hebrews 12:17; Tehillim - Psalms 78:34"> And you2f returned and wept before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); but the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) would not hearken to your voice, nor give ear unto you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46" data-xref="Numbers 20:1; Numbers 20:22; Judges 11:16–17"> So you2f abode in Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93; many days, according unto the days that you2f abode &#91;there&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:1; Joshua 22:7; Numbers 33:17–18"> Then we turned, and took our journey into the wilderness by the way of the Red sea, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto me: and we compassed mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93; many days.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Numbers 32:8; Numbers 13:26; Deuteronomy 9:23"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Numbers 33:38; Deuteronomy 4:1–2; Numbers 20:1"> You2f have compassed this mountain long enough: turn you northward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 21:21–35; Joshua 12:2–6; Joshua 13:10–12"> And command youi the people, saying, You2f &#91;are&#93; to pass through the coast of your brethren the children of ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93;, which dwell in Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;; and they shall be afraid of you: take you2f good heed unto yourselves therefore:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:8; Deuteronomy 32:46; Deuteronomy 31:9"> Meddle not with them; for I will not give you of their land, no, not so much as a foot breadth; because I have given mount Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93; unto ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93; &#91;for&#93; a possession.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 3:1; Numbers 10:11–13; Exodus 19:1–2"> You2f shall buy meat of them for money, that you2f may eat; and you2f shall also buy water of them for money, that you2f may drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Deuteronomy 11:24; Joshua 10:40; Amos 2:9"> For the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) youi in all the works of youi hand: he knows youi walking through this great wilderness: these forty years the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;has been&#93; with youi; youi have lacked nothing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 15:18; Genesis 12:7; Genesis 26:3–4"> And when we passed by from our brethren the children of ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93;, which dwelt in Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, through the way of the plain from Eilat &#91;Grove of Lofty Trees&#93;, and from ‘Etzyon-Gever &#91;Backbone of a Man&#93;, we turned and passed by the way of the wilderness of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Exodus 18:18; Numbers 11:17; Numbers 11:11–14"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, Distress not the Mo’avi &#91;of his father&#93;, neither contend with them in battle: for I will not give youi of their land &#91;for&#93; a possession; because I have given ‘Ar &#91;A City&#93; unto the children of Lot &#91;for&#93; a possession.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Exodus 32:13; Genesis 22:17"> The Emim &#91;Terrors&#93; dwelt in it in times past, a people great, and many, and tall, as the ‘Anakim &#91;Longed-Necked&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 115:14; Genesis 22:17; Numbers 6:27"> Which also were accounted giants, as the ‘Anakim &#91;Longed-Necked&#93;; but the Mo’avi &#91;of his father&#93; call them Emim &#91;Terrors&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="2 Corinthians 3:5; 1 Kings 3:7–9; 2 Corinthians 2:16"> The Horim &#91;Cave Dweller&#93; also dwelt in Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93; formerly; but the children of ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93; succeeded them, when they had destroyed them from before them, and dwelt in their stead; as Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; did unto the land of his possession, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) gave unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Exodus 18:21; Acts 1:21–23; Numbers 11:16–17"> Now rise up, &#91;said I&#93;, and get you over the brook Zered &#91;Osier Brook&#93;. And we went over the brook Zered &#91;Osier Brook&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> And the space in which we came from Kadesh &#91;Holy&#93; -Barnea &#91;Holy&#93;, until we were come over the brook Zered &#91;Osier Brook&#93;, &#91;was&#93; thirty and eight years; until all the generation of the men of war were wasted out from among the host, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) swore unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:18; Exodus 18:25–26; Ephesians 4:11"> For indeed the hand of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was against them, to destroy them from among the host, until they were consumed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="John 7:24; Exodus 22:21; 2 Chronicles 19:6–10"> So it came to pass, when all the men of war were consumed and dead from among the people,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:19; Exodus 18:26; Jacob 2:9"> That the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto me, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Deuteronomy 12:32; Deuteronomy 4:5; Acts 20:20"> youi are to pass over through ‘Ar &#91;A City&#93;, the coast of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, this day:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:2; Deuteronomy 8:15; Jeremiah 2:6"> And &#91;when&#93; youi come near over against the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93;, distress them not, nor meddle with them: for I will not give youi of the land of the children of ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; &#91;any&#93; possession; because I have given it unto the children of Lot &#91;for&#93; a possession.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:7–8"> (That also was accounted a land of giants: giants dwelt in it in old time; and the ‘Amon &#91;Inbred&#93; call them Zamzumim &#91;Plotters&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Tehillim - Psalms 46:1; Numbers 14:8–9"> A people great, and many, and tall, as the ‘Anakim &#91;Longed-Necked&#93;; but the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) destroyed them before them; and they succeeded them, and dwelt in their stead:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 13:1–20"> As he did to the children of ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93;, which dwelt in Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, when he destroyed the Horim &#91;Cave Dweller&#93; from before them; and they succeeded them, and dwelt in their stead even unto this day:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 13:3–33"> And the ‘Avim &#91;Ruins&#93; &#91;Ruins&#93; which dwelt in Hatzerim &#91;Villages&#93;, &#91;even&#93; unto ‘Azah &#91;The Strong&#93;, the Kaftorim &#91;A Crown&#93;, which came forth out of Kaftor &#91;A Crown&#93;, destroyed them, and dwelt in their stead.)</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 13:21–27; Joshua 2:1–2"> Rise you2f up, take your journey, and pass over the river ‘Arnon &#91;Rushing Stream&#93;: behold, I have given into youi hand Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; the Emori &#91;Rebellious Highlanders&#93;, king of Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93;, and his land: begin to possess &#91;it&#93;, and contend with him in battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> This day will I begin to put the dread of youi and the fear of youi upon the nations &#91;that are&#93; under the whole heaven, who shall hear report of youi, and shall tremble, and be in anguish because of youi.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Deuteronomy 9:28; Tehillim - Psalms 106:25; Numbers 14:3"> Let me pass through youi land: I will go along by the high way, I will neither turn unto the right hand nor to the left.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Numbers 13:28–33; Deuteronomy 9:1–2; Joshua 15:14"> youi shall sell me meat for money, that I may eat; and give me water for money, that I may drink: only I will pass through on my feet;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> (As the children of ‘Esav &#91;Hairy&#93; which dwell in Se’ir &#91;Shaggy&#93;, and the Mo’avi &#91;of his father&#93; which dwell in ‘Ar &#91;A City&#93;, did unto me;) until I shall pass over Yarden &#91;Descender&#93; into the land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; gives us.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30" data-xref="Exodus 14:14; Nehemiah 4:20; Romans 8:31"> But Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; king of Heshbon &#91;Stronghold&#93; would not let us pass by him: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; hardened his spirit, and made his heart obstinate, that he might deliver him into youi hand, as &#91;appears&#93; this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31" data-xref="Isaiah 46:3–4; Acts 13:18; Hosea 11:3–4"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, Behold, I have begun to give Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; and his land before youi: begin to possess, that youi may inherit his land.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36" data-xref="Numbers 14:24; Joshua 14:6–14; Numbers 32:12"> From ‘Aeo’er &#91;Ruins&#93;, which &#91;is&#93; by the brink of the river of ‘Arnon &#91;Rushing Stream&#93;, and &#91;from&#93; the city that &#91;is&#93; by the river, even unto Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;, there was not one city too strong for us: the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; delivered all unto us:</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:7–8"> Until the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) have given rest unto your brethren, as well as unto you, and &#91;until&#93; they also possess the land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has given them beyond Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;: and &#91;then&#93; shall you2f return every man unto his possession, which I have given you.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Tehillim - Psalms 46:1; Numbers 14:8–9"> And I commanded Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; at that time, saying, You1s eyes have seen all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has done unto these two kings: so shall the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) do unto all the kingdoms to which youi pass.</span>

<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 13:1–20"> You2f shall not fear them: for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; he shall fight for you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 13:3–33"> And I besought the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) at that time, saying,</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> I pray youi, let me go over, and see the good land that &#91;is&#93; beyond Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, that goodly mountain, and L’vanon &#91;Whiteness&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Numbers 14:1–4; Acts 7:51; Tehillim - Psalms 106:24–25"> But the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) was exceedingly angry with me for your sakes, and would not hear me: and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, Let it suffice youi; speak no more unto me of this matter.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Numbers 13:28–33; Deuteronomy 9:1–2; Joshua 15:14"> But charge Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93;, and encourage him, and strengthen him: for he shall go over before this people, and he shall cause them to inherit the land which youi shall see.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> So we abode in the valley over against Beit-P’or &#91;House of the Cleft (split hoof; an opening, break or split)&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:1; Joshua 22:7; Numbers 33:17–18"> Now therefore hearken, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, unto the statutes and unto the judgments, which I teach you, for to do &#91;them&#93;, that you2f may live, and go in and possess the land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of your fathers gives you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Numbers 32:8; Numbers 13:26; Deuteronomy 9:23"> You2f shall not add unto the word which I command you, neither shall you2f diminish &#91;ought&#93; from it, that you2f may keep the commandments of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; which I command you.</span>

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 21:21–35; Joshua 12:2–6; Joshua 13:10–12"> But you2f that did cling to (hold onto; stick to) the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;are&#93; alive every one of you this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:8; Deuteronomy 32:46; Deuteronomy 31:9"> Behold, I have taught you statutes and judgments, even as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; commanded me, that you2f should do so in the land to which you2f go to possess it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 3:1; Numbers 10:11–13; Exodus 19:1–2"> Keep therefore and do &#91;them&#93;; for this &#91;is&#93; your wisdom and your understanding in the sight of the nations, which shall hear all these statutes, and say, Surely this great nation &#91;is&#93; a wise and understanding people.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Deuteronomy 11:24; Joshua 10:40; Amos 2:9"> For what nation &#91;is there so&#93; great, who &#91;has&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;so&#93; near unto them, as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; in all &#91;things that&#93; we call upon him &#91;for&#93;?</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Exodus 18:18; Numbers 11:17; Numbers 11:11–14"> Only take heed to yourself, and keep youi soul diligently, lest youi forget the things which youi eyes have seen, and lest they depart from youi heart all the days of youi life: but teach them youi sons, and youi sons’ sons;</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="2 Corinthians 3:5; 1 Kings 3:7–9; 2 Corinthians 2:16"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto you out of the midst of the fire: you2f heard the voice of the words, but saw no similitude; only &#91;you2f heard&#93; a voice.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:18; Exodus 18:25–26; Ephesians 4:11"> Take you2f therefore good heed unto yourselves; for you2f saw no manner of similitude on the day &#91;that&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto you in Horev &#91;Desert&#93; out of the midst of the fire:</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="John 7:24; Exodus 22:21; 2 Chronicles 19:6–10"> Lest you2f corrupt &#91;yourselves&#93;, and make you a graven image, the similitude of any figure, the likeness of male or female,</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Tehillim - Psalms 46:1; Numbers 14:8–9"> Neither shall youi desire youi neighbour’s wife, neither shall youi covet youi neighbour’s house, his field, or his manservant, or his maidservant, his ox, or his ass, or any &#91;thing&#93; that &#91;is&#93; youi neighbour’s.</span>

And he wrote them in two tables of stone, and delivered them unto me.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 13:21–27; Joshua 2:1–2"> And you2f said, Behold, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has showed us his glory and his greatness, and we have heard his voice out of the midst of the fire: we have seen this day that God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; does talk with man, and he lives.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Numbers 14:1–4; Acts 7:51; Tehillim - Psalms 106:24–25"> For who &#91;is there of&#93; all flesh, that has heard the voice of the living God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; speaking out of the midst of the fire, as we &#91;have&#93;, and lived?</span>

<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Deuteronomy 9:28; Tehillim - Psalms 106:25; Numbers 14:3"> Go youi near, and hear all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; shall say: and speak youi unto us all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; shall speak unto youi; and we will hear &#91;it&#93;, and do &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Numbers 13:28–33; Deuteronomy 9:1–2; Joshua 15:14"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) heard the voice of your words, when you2f spoke unto me; and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto me, I have heard the voice of the words of this people, which they have spoken unto youi: they have well said all that they have spoken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:1; Joshua 22:7; Numbers 33:17–18"> These &#91;are&#93; the words of the covenant, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; to make with the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in the land of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, beside the covenant which he made with them in Horev &#91;Desert&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:7–8"> For when I shall have brought them into the land which I swore unto their fathers, that flows with milk and honey; and they shall have eaten and filled themselves, and waxen fat; then will they turn unto other gods, and serve them, and provoke me, and break my covenant.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="2 Corinthians 3:5; 1 Kings 3:7–9; 2 Corinthians 2:16"> &#91;And&#93; of Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93; he said, The beloved of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall dwell in safety by him; &#91;and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate)&#93; shall cover him all the day long, and he shall dwell between his shoulders.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Exodus 18:21; Acts 1:21–23; Numbers 11:16–17"> And of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; he said, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;be&#93; his land, for the precious things of heaven, for the dew, and for the deep that couches beneath,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> And for the precious fruits &#91;brought forth&#93; by the sun, and for the precious things put forth by the moon,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:18; Exodus 18:25–26; Ephesians 4:11"> And for the chief things of the ancient mountains, and for the precious things of the lasting hills,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="John 7:24; Exodus 22:21; 2 Chronicles 19:6–10"> And for the precious things of the earth and fullness thereof, and &#91;for&#93; the good will of him that dwelt in the bush: let &#91;the blessing&#93; come upon the head of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, and upon the top of the head of him &#91;that was&#93; separated from his brethren.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Deuteronomy 16:19; Exodus 18:26; Jacob 2:9"> His glory &#91;is like&#93; the firstling of his bullock, and his horns &#91;are like&#93; the horns of unicorns: with them he shall push the people together to the ends of the earth: and they &#91;are&#93; the ten thousands of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and they &#91;are&#93; the thousands of M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Deuteronomy 12:32; Deuteronomy 4:5; Acts 20:20"> And of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93; he said, Rejoice, Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, in youi going out; and, Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, in youi tents.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:2; Deuteronomy 8:15; Jeremiah 2:6"> They shall call the people unto the mountain; there they shall offer sacrifices of righteousness: for they shall suck &#91;of&#93; the abundance of the seas, and &#91;of&#93; treasures hid in the sand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Deuteronomy 1:7–8"> And of Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93; he said, Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; he that enlarges Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;: he dwells as a lion, and tears the arm with the crown of the head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="Joshua 1:9; Tehillim - Psalms 46:1; Numbers 14:8–9"> And he provided the first part for himself, because there, &#91;in&#93; a portion of the lawgiver, &#91;was he&#93; seated; and he came with the heads of the people, he executed the justice of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and his judgments with Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Numbers 13:1–20"> And of Dan &#91;Judge&#93; he said, Dan &#91;Judge&#93; &#91;is&#93; a lion’s whelp (a cub; a young one): he shall leap from Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23" data-xref="Numbers 13:3–33"> And of Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93; he said, O Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, satisfied with favour, and full with the blessing of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): possess youi the west and the south.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24" data-xref="Numbers 13:21–27; Joshua 2:1–2"> And of Asher &#91;Happy&#93; he said, &#91;Let&#93; Asher &#91;Happy&#93; &#91;be&#93; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) with children; let him be acceptable to his brethren, and let him dip his foot in oil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> You1s shoes &#91;shall be&#93; iron and brass; and as youi days, &#91;so shall&#93; youi strength &#91;be&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26" data-xref="Numbers 14:1–4; Acts 7:51; Tehillim - Psalms 106:24–25"> &#91;There is&#93; none like unto the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yeshurun &#91;Upright One&#93;, &#91;who&#93; rides upon the heaven in youi help, and in his excellency on the sky.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27" data-xref="Deuteronomy 9:28; Tehillim - Psalms 106:25; Numbers 14:3"> The eternal God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is youi&#93; refuge, and underneath &#91;are&#93; the everlasting arms: and he shall thrust out the enemy from before youi; and shall say, Destroy &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28" data-xref="Numbers 13:28–33; Deuteronomy 9:1–2; Joshua 15:14"> Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; then shall dwell in safety alone: the fountain of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; &#91;shall be&#93; upon a land of corn and wine; also his heavens shall drop down dew.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Happy &#91;are&#93; youi, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: who &#91;is&#93; like unto youi, O people saved by the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), the shield of youi help, and who &#91;is&#93; the sword of youi excellency! and youi enemies shall be found liars unto youi; and youi shall trample upon their high places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Samuel 25:1; Joshua 22:7; Numbers 33:17–18"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; went up from the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; unto the mountain of Nevo &#91;Prophet&#93;, to the top of Pisgah &#91;Cleft (split hoof; an opening, break or split) Mountain Range&#93;, that &#91;is&#93; over against Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;. And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) showed him all the land of Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93;, unto Dan &#91;Judge&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Numbers 32:8; Numbers 13:26; Deuteronomy 9:23"> And all Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, and the land of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93;, and all the land of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, unto the utmost sea,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Numbers 33:38; Deuteronomy 4:1–2; Numbers 20:1"> And the south &#91;Negev&#93;, and the plain &#91;‘Aravah&#93; of the valley of Yericho &#91;Its Moon&#93;, the city of palm trees, unto Tzo’ar &#91;Insignificance&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Numbers 21:21–35; Joshua 12:2–6; Joshua 13:10–12"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto him, This &#91;is&#93; the land which I swore unto Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, unto Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and unto Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, saying, I will give it unto youi seed: I have caused youi to see &#91;it&#93; with youi eyes, but youi shall not go over there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:8; Deuteronomy 32:46; Deuteronomy 31:9"> So Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; the servant of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) died there in the land of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, according to the word of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Exodus 3:1; Numbers 10:11–13; Exodus 19:1–2"> And he buried him in a valley in the land of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93;, over against Beit-P’or &#91;House of the Cleft (split hoof; an opening, break or split)&#93;: but no man knows of his sepulcher unto this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Deuteronomy 11:24; Joshua 10:40; Amos 2:9"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; &#91;was&#93; an hundred and twenty years old when he died: his eye was not dim, nor his natural force abated.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Genesis 15:18; Genesis 12:7; Genesis 26:3–4"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; wept for Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; in the plains of Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; thirty days: so the days of weeping &#91;and&#93; mourning for Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; were ended.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Exodus 18:18; Numbers 11:17; Numbers 11:11–14"> And Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; the son of Nun &#91;Posterity&#93; was full of the spirit of wisdom; for Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; had laid his hands upon him: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; listened unto him, and did as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Exodus 32:13; Genesis 22:17"> And there arose not a prophet since in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; like unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) knew face to face,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 115:14; Genesis 22:17; Numbers 6:27"> In all the signs and the wonders, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) sent him to do in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; to Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and to all his servants, and to all his land,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="2 Corinthians 3:5; 1 Kings 3:7–9; 2 Corinthians 2:16"> And in all that mighty hand, and in all the great terror which Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; showed in the sight of all Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
</div>
